The system has been deployed in production at global scale and applied to downstream surfaces including content ranking and search, driving measurable improvements in content freshness and user experience.

</details>


### [185] [RAQG-QPP: Query Performance Prediction with Retrieved Query Variants and Retrieval Augmented Query Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27244)
*Fangzheng Tian,Debasis Ganguly,Craig Macdonald*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出RAQG方法，通过从历史查询日志中检索查询变体（QVs），并利用大语言模型（LLMs）基于检索到的QVs生成额外查询变体，来改进神经排序模型的查询性能预测（QPP），在TREC DL'19和DL'20上相比现有最优QV-based方法在MonoT5等神经排序模型上提升高达30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QV-based的QPP方法依赖输入查询的词项扩展生成查询变体，容易产生不连贯、幻觉性和偏离主题的查询变体，导致预测效果不佳；且神经排序模型的QPP性能弱于词汇检索模型。

Method: 1) 从历史查询日志中检索与输入查询相关的真实查询作为QVs；2) 利用LLMs基于检索到的QVs生成额外的查询变体，克服仅依赖日志中现有查询的局限性；3) 将两种QVs用于增强QPP。

Result: 在TREC DL'19和DL'20数据集上，RAQG增强的QPP在神经排序模型（如MonoT5）上比现有最优QV-based方法提升高达30%。

Conclusion: 结合历史查询日志检索和LLM生成查询变体的策略能显著提升神经排序模型的QPP性能，有效克服传统词项扩展生成不相关QVs的缺陷。

Abstract: Query Performance Prediction (QPP) estimates the retrieval quality of ranking models without the use of any human-assessed relevance judgements, and finds applications in query-specific selective decision making to improve overall retrieval effectiveness. Although unsupervised QPP approaches are effective for lexical retrieval models, they usually perform weaker for neural rankers. Recent work shows that leveraging query variants (QVs), i.e., queries with potentially similar information needs to a given query, can enhance unsupervised QPP accuracy. However, existing QV-based prediction methods rely on query variants generated by term expansion of the input query, which is likely to yield incoherent, hallucinatory and off-topic QVs. In this paper, we propose to make use of queries retrieved from a log of past queries as QVs to be subsequently used for QPP. In addition to directly applying retrieved QVs in QPP, we further propose to leverage large language models (LLMs) to generate QVs conditioned on the retrieved QVs, thus mitigating the limitation of relying only on existing queries in a log. Experiments on TREC DL'19 and DL'20 show that QPP enhanced with RAQG outperform the best-performing existing QV-based prediction approach by as much as 30% on neural ranking models such as MonoT5.

</details>


### [186] [NuggetIndex: Governed Atomic Retrieval for Maintainable RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27306)
*Saber Zerhoudi,Michael Granitzer,Jelena Mitrovic*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出NuggetIndex系统，将原子信息单元（nuggets）作为可管理记录存储，通过时间有效性、生命周期状态和证据链接来管理，解决了RAG系统中检索对象与评估对象不匹配的问题，在多个任务上提升了召回率、时间正确性并降低了冲突率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统通常检索段落或静态命题，而评估则基于事实性指标，这种检索对象与评估对象之间的单位不匹配导致：1）语料库演进时维护困难；2）无法捕捉被取代的事实或来源冲突。

Method: 提出NuggetIndex系统，将原子信息单元（nuggets）作为可管理记录存储，每个记录维护证据链接、时间有效性区间和生命周期状态。在排序前过滤无效或废弃的nuggets，防止过时信息被纳入生成过程。

Result: 在nuggetized MS MARCO子集、时间Wikipedia QA数据集和多跳QA任务上，相比段落检索和未管理的命题检索基线：nugget召回率提升42%，时间正确性提升9个百分点且未出现时间过滤基线中的召回崩溃，冲突率降低55%。生成器输入长度减少64%。

Conclusion: NuggetIndex通过原子化的信息单元管理，有效解决了RAG系统中检索与评估之间的单位不匹配问题，同时支持轻量级索引结构，适用于浏览器端和资源受限的部署场景。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems are frequently evaluated via fact-based metrics, yet standard implementations retrieve passages or static propositions. This unit mismatch between evaluation and retrieval objects hinders maintenance when corpora evolve and fails to capture superseded facts or source disagreements. We propose NuggetIndex, a retrieval system that stores atomic information units as managed records, so called nuggets. Each record maintains links to evidence, a temporal validity interval, and a lifecycle state. By filtering invalid or deprecated nuggets prior to ranking, the system prevents the inclusion of outdated information. We evaluate the approach using a nuggetized MS MARCO subset, a temporal Wikipedia QA dataset, and a multi-hop QA task. Against passage and unmanaged proposition retrieval baselines, NuggetIndex improves nugget recall by 42%, increases temporal correctness by 9 percentage points without the recall collapse observed in time-filtered baselines, and reduces conflict rates by 55%. The compact nugget format reduces generator input length by 64% while enabling lightweight index structures suitable for browser-based and resource-constrained deployment. We release our implementation, datasets, and evaluation scripts

</details>


### [187] [From Unstructured to Structured: LLM-Guided Attribute Graphs for Entity Search and Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27410)
*Yilun Zhu,Nikhita Vedula,Shervin Malmasi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合大语言模型（LLM）驱动属性图构建与图感知LLM排序的两阶段方法，用于电商实体搜索。离线阶段从非结构化文本中提取结构化产品属性并构建可复用的类别感知属性图；在线阶段基于该结构化表示而非原始文本对候选结果进行排序，每产品token使用量减少57%，排序精度提升。零样本场景下平均精度提升超过5%，且无需训练数据，跨品类泛化性强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于嵌入的方法难以捕获电商场景中不同品类和上下文下细粒度的、与上下文相关的属性重要性，导致实体搜索结果不够精准。

Method: 两阶段方法：(1) 离线阶段：利用LLM从非结构化文本中提取结构化产品属性，构建具有类别感知模式的属性图；(2) 在线阶段：采用图感知的LLM对检索到的候选结果进行排序，基于结构化属性表示而非原始文本进行推理。

Result: 每产品token使用量减少57%，零样本场景下平均精度提升超过5%，无需训练数据，跨品类泛化能力强。

Conclusion: 该方法在零样本场景下优于多个基线方法，展现了实际部署的巨大潜力，有效解决了电商实体搜索中上下文相关属性重要性捕捉的难题。

Abstract: Entity search, i.e., finding the most similar entities to a query entity, faces unique challenges in e-commerce, where product similarity varies across categories and contexts. Traditional embedding-based approaches often struggle to capture nuanced context-specific attribute relevance. In this paper, we present a two-stage approach combining Large Language Model (LLM)-driven attribute graph construction with graph-aware LLM ranking. In the offline stage, we extract structured product attributes from unstructured text, and construct a reusable attribute graph with category-aware schemas. In the online stage, we rank retrieved candidates by reasoning over this structured representation rather than raw text, reducing per-product token usage by 57% while improving ranking precision. Experiments show that our approach outperforms multiple baselines under zero-shot scenarios, achieving a over 5% improvement in average precision without requiring training data, generalizes robustly across diverse product categories, and shows immense potential for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [188] [A Reproducibility Study of LLM-Based Query Reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27421)
*Amin Bigdeli,Radin Hamidi Rad,Hai Son Le,Mert Incesu,Negar Arabzadeh,Charles L. A. Clarke,Ebrahim Bagheri*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文在统一可控的实验框架下，系统复现并比较了十种基于LLM的查询改写方法，发现改写增益强烈依赖于检索范式，且大模型并非总能带来更好性能。作者开源了QueryGym工具包与排行榜以支持透明复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的查询改写研究在异构实验条件下进行，结论的可复现性和对具体实现选择的依赖程度尚不明确，亟需系统性的比较研究。

Method: 在统一严格控制的实验框架下，对十种代表性LLM查询改写方法进行系统复现与比较，涵盖两种架构家族、两种参数规模、三种检索范式（词项、学习型稀疏、稠密）及九个基准数据集。

Result: 查询改写的增益强烈受限于检索范式；在词项检索下观察到的改进不能一致地迁移到神经检索器上；更大的LLM并不均匀地带来更好的下游性能。

Conclusion: 这些发现澄清了先前工作中报告增益的稳定性与局限；通过QueryGym开源工具包与公开排行榜支持透明复现与持续比较。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are now widely used for query reformulation and expansion in Information Retrieval, with many studies reporting substantial effectiveness gains. However, these results are typically obtained under heterogeneous experimental conditions, making it difficult to assess which findings are reproducible and which depend on specific implementation choices. In this work, we present a systematic reproducibility and comparative study of ten representative LLM-based query reformulation methods under a unified and strictly controlled experimental framework. We evaluate methods across two architectural LLM families at two parameter scales, three retrieval paradigms (lexical, learned sparse, and dense), and nine benchmark datasets spanning TREC Deep Learning and BEIR. Our results show that reformulation gains are strongly conditioned on the retrieval paradigm, that improvements observed under lexical retrieval do not consistently transfer to neural retrievers, and that larger LLMs do not uniformly yield better downstream performance. These findings clarify the stability and limits of reported gains in prior work. To enable transparent replication and ongoing comparison, we release all prompts, configurations, evaluation scripts, and run files through QueryGym, an open-source reformulation toolkit with a public leaderboard.\footnote{https://leaderboard.querygym.com}

</details>


### [189] [Reproducing Adaptive Reranking for Reasoning-Intensive IR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27577)
*Mandeep Rathee,V Venktesh,Sean MacAvaney,Avishek Anand*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文复现了GAR（基于图的适应性重排序）方法在推理密集型检索基准BRIGHT上的表现，通过在重排序过程中迭代探索语料图来缓解检索-重排序级联管道的有限召回问题。实验表明，GAR能在几乎不增加计算开销的情况下提升多种模型的推理密集型检索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的检索-重排序级联管道存在有限召回问题，现有方法主要通过改进第一阶段检索器来解决，但计算成本高昂。作者希望在不增加大量计算开销的前提下，通过改进重排序过程本身来缓解该问题。

Method: 复现GAR方法，通过迭代探索语料图结构来修改重排序过程；在BRIGHT推理密集型检索基准上测试，使用推理型和非推理型重排序模型进行评估。

Result: GAR能够提升多种模型在推理密集型检索任务上的有效性，且计算开销极小。重排序器的信号质量对于在语料图中识别更多相关文档起关键作用。

Conclusion: 本研究使得能够处理推理密集型查询的检索系统在实际部署中更加可行。

Abstract: The classical cascading pipeline of retrieve--rerank suffers from a bounded recall problem, stemming from limitations of the first-stage retriever. Most current approaches address the bounded recall problem by improving the first-stage retriever, but this incurs substantial training and inference costs, especially to handle queries that require substantial reasoning. To circumvent the computational costs of reasoning-based retrievers, we replicate the findings of GAR, Graph-based Adaptive Reranking, on the BRIGHT reasoning-intensive retrieval benchmark. GAR addresses the bounded recall problem by modifying the reranking process itself through iterative exploration of a corpus graph, but it was previously only tested on models designed for topical and question-answering-style queries. Hence, reproduce GAR in reasoning-intensive settings with reasoning and non-reasoning reranking models. We observe that the quality of the reranker's signal plays an important role in identifying additional relevant documents within the corpus graph. Overall, we find that GAR boosts the effectiveness of reasoning-intensive retrieval across a variety of models while contributing minimally to computational overheads. Ultimately, this work enables more practical deployment of retrieval systems that can address reasoning-intensive queries.

</details>


### [190] [One Pass, Any Order: Position-Invariant Listwise Reranking for LLM-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27599)
*Ethan Bito,Yongli Ren,Estrid He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对LLM在推荐重排序中对候选顺序敏感的问题，提出InvariRank框架，通过架构级设计（结构化注意力掩码和共享位置编码）实现置换不变性，在保持性能的同时产生稳定排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在列表式推荐重排序中，由于decoder-only架构的序列计算特性，候选集排列顺序会影响最终评分和排序结果，导致排序反映的是提示序列化方式而非用户真实偏好，使得LLM重排序器不可靠。

Method: 提出InvariRank框架：(1) 通过结构化注意力掩码阻断候选间的交叉注意力；(2) 在RoPE下通过共享位置编码消除位置引起的评分变化；(3) 结合列表式学习排序目标，单次前向传播即可对所有候选评分，无需排列不变性训练目标。

Result: 在推荐基准测试上，InvariRank在保持竞争性排序效果的同时，实现了跨候选排列的稳定排序。

Conclusion: 架构级别的置换不变性是实现可靠且高效LLM推荐重排序的实用路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for recommendation reranking, but their listwise predictions can depend on the order in which candidates are presented. This creates a mismatch between the set-based nature of recommendation and the sequence-based computation of decoder-only LLMs, where permuting an otherwise identical candidate set can change item scores and final rankings. Such order sensitivity makes LLM-based rerankers difficult to rely on, since rankings may reflect prompt serialization rather than user preference. We propose InvariRank, a permutation-invariant listwise reranking framework that addresses this dependence at the architectural level. InvariRank blocks cross-candidate attention with a structured attention mask and negates position-induced scoring changes through shared positional framing under Rotary Positional Embeddings (RoPE). Combined with a listwise learning-to-rank objective, the model scores all candidates in a single forward pass, avoiding permutation-based invariance training objectives that require multiple permutations of a candidate set. Experiments on recommendation benchmarks show that InvariRank maintains competitive ranking effectiveness while producing stable rankings across candidate permutations. The results suggest that architectural invariance is a practical route to reliable and efficient LLM-based recommendation reranking. The source code is at https://github.com/ejbito/InvariRank.

</details>


### [191] [Purifying Multimodal Retrieval: Fragment-Level Evidence Selection for RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27600)
*Xihang Wang,Zihan Wang,Chengkai Huang,Cao Liu,Ke Zeng,Quan Z. Sheng,Lina Yao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: FES-RAG将多模态RAG从文档级检索重构为细粒度片段级证据选择，通过句子级文本片段和区域级视觉片段，利用片段信息增益(FIG)指标指导选择，在M2RAG基准上取得最高27% CIDEr相对提升，同时减少上下文长度并提高事实准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MRAG框架将检索到的证据视为不可分割的文档，假设文档内所有内容同等重要，但实际上只有小部分与查询相关，其余部分引入噪声导致性能下降。

Method: 提出FES-RAG框架：1) 将多模态文档分解为句子级文本片段和区域级视觉片段；2) 提出Fragment Information Gain (FIG)指标衡量每个片段对MLLM生成置信度的边际贡献；3) 基于FIG从高容量MLLM蒸馏片段级效用判断到轻量选择器。

Result: 在M2RAG基准上，FES-RAG持续优于文档级MRAG方法，最高实现27% CIDEr相对提升，同时选择更少但信息更丰富的片段，缩短上下文长度并提高事实准确性和生成连贯性。

Conclusion: 将MRAG重构为细粒度证据选择问题有效解决了文档级噪声问题，FIG指标和蒸馏机制实现了高效准确的片段选择。

Abstract: Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (MRAG) is widely adopted for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) with external evidence to reduce hallucinations. Despite its success, most existing MRAG frameworks treat retrieved evidence as indivisible documents, implicitly assuming that all content within a document is equally informative. In practice, however, sometimes only a small fraction of a document is relevant to a given query, while the remaining content introduces substantial noise that may lead to performance degradation. We address this fundamental limitation by reframing MRAG as a fine-grained evidence selection problem. We propose Fragment-level Evidence Selection for RAG (FES-RAG), a framework that selects atomic multimodal fragments rather than entire documents as grounding evidence. FES-RAG decomposes retrieved multimodal documents into sentence-level textual fragments and region-level visual fragments, enabling precise identification of evidence that directly supports generation. To guide fragment selection, we introduce Fragment Information Gain (FIG), a principled metric that measures the marginal contribution of each fragment to the MLLM's generation confidence. Based on FIG, we distill fragment-level utility judgments from a high-capacity MLLM into a lightweight selector, achieving accurate evidence selection with low inference overhead. Experiments on the M2RAG benchmark show that FES-RAG consistently outperforms state-of-the-art document-level MRAG methods, achieving up to 27 percent relative improvement in CIDEr. By selecting fewer yet more informative fragments, our approach substantially reduces context length while improving factual accuracy and generation coherence.

</details>


### [192] [Position-Aware Drafting for Inference Acceleration in LLM-Based Generative List-Wise Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27747)
*Jiaju Chen,Chongming Gao,Chenxiao Fan,Haoyan Liu,Qingpeng Cai,Peng Jiang,Xiangnan He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出PAD-Rec模块，通过物品位置嵌入和步骤位置嵌入增强推测解码中的草稿模型，在生成式推荐中实现高达3.1倍加速，相比强基线平均提升约5%的加速增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的生成式列表推荐中，推测解码（SD）的草稿模型未考虑两个关键事实：token的语义依赖于其在物品内的位置槽位，以及不确定性随推测深度增加。这种统一处理方式限制了SD的加速效果。

Method: 提出PAD-Rec轻量模块，在草稿模型中注入两种互补信号：1) 物品位置嵌入（Item position embeddings），编码每个token在物品内的槽位，增强结构感知；2) 步骤位置嵌入（Step position embeddings），编码推测步骤，使模型适应深度依赖的不确定性。此外设计简单门控机制（可学习系数用于物品槽位、上下文驱动门用于推测步骤）来调和这些信号与基础特征。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上实现最高3.1倍实际加速，相比强SD基线平均获得约5%的加速增益，同时基本保持推荐质量。

Conclusion: PAD-Rec是一种可训练、易于集成、推理开销极小的位置感知推测解码模块，能有效提升生成式推荐的推理效率。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based generative list-wise recommendation has advanced rapidly, but decoding remains sequential and thus latency-prone. To accelerate inference without changing the target distribution, speculative decoding (SD) uses a small draft model to propose several next tokens at once and a target LLM to verify and accept the longest prefix, skipping multiple steps per round. In generative recommendation, however, each item is represented by multiple semantic-ID tokens, often with separators, and current drafts typically treat these tokens uniformly. This overlooks two practical facts: (i) a token's semantics depend on its within-item slot, and (ii) uncertainty tends to increase with speculation depth. Without modeling these effects, SD's speedups can be limited. We introduce PAD-Rec, Position-Aware Drafting for generative Recommendation, a lightweight module that augments the draft model with two complementary signals. Item position embeddings explicitly encode the within-item slot of each token, strengthening structural awareness. Step position embeddings encode the draft step, allowing the model to adapt to depth-dependent uncertainty and improve proposal quality. To harmonize these signals with base features, we add simple gates: a learnable coefficient for item slots and a context-driven gate for draft steps. The module is trainable, easy to integrate with standard draft models, and adds negligible inference overhead. Extensive experiments on four real-world datasets show up to 3.1x wall-clock speedup and about 5% average wall-clock speedup gain over strong SD baselines, while largely preserving recommendation quality.

</details>


### [193] [NeocorRAG: Less Irrelevant Information, More Explicit Evidence, and More Effective Recall via Evidence Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27852)
*Shiyao Peng,Qianhe Zheng,Zhuodi Hao,Zichen Tang,Rongjin Li,Qing Huang,Jiayu Huang,Jiacheng Liu,Yifan Zhu,Haihong E*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Recall Conversion Rate (RCR)指标量化检索对推理的贡献，发现检索性能提升与下游推理增益之间存在衰减现象；为此提出NeocorRAG框架，通过挖掘证据链实现检索质量优化，在多个基准上达到SOTA且仅需不到20%的token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG方法中，检索性能提升未能一致地转化为下游推理能力的提升，存在"检索-推理"间的增益衰减问题；同时已有方法要么忽视检索质量，要么只关注质量优化而牺牲召回率，缺乏对检索质量的全面理解。

Method: 提出RCR指标量化检索对推理的贡献；设计NeocorRAG框架，包含三步：1) 激活搜索算法获取精炼候选空间；2) 约束解码生成精确证据链；3) 利用证据链集引导检索优化过程。

Result: 在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA、MuSiQue、NQ基准上，NeocorRAG在3B和70B参数模型上均达到SOTA，且token消耗仅为同类方法的20%以下。

Conclusion: 提出了一种高效、无需训练、兼顾检索质量与召回率的RAG增强范式，有效弥合了检索性能与下游推理之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Although precise recall is a core objective in Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), a critical oversight persists in the field: improvements in retrieval performance do not consistently translate to commensurate gains in downstream reasoning. To diagnose this gap, we propose the Recall Conversion Rate (RCR), a novel evaluation metric to quantify the contribution of retrieval to reasoning accuracy. Our quantitative analysis of mainstream RAG methods reveals that as Recall@5 improves, the RCR exhibits a near-linear decay. We identify the neglect of retrieval quality in these methods as the underlying cause. In contrast, approaches that focus solely on quality optimization often suffer from inferior recall performance. Both categories lack a comprehensive understanding of retrieval quality optimization, resulting in a trade-off dilemma. To address these challenges, we propose comprehensive retrieval quality optimization criteria and introduce the NeocorRAG framework. This framework achieves holistic retrieval quality optimization by systematically mining and utilizing Evidence Chains. Specifically, NeocorRAG first employs an innovative activated search algorithm to obtain a refined candidate space. Then it ensures precise evidence chain generation through constrained decoding. Finally, the retrieved set of evidence chains guides the retrieval optimization process. Evaluated on benchmarks including HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA, MuSiQue, and NQ, NeocorRAG achieves SOTA performance on both 3B and 70B parameter models, while consuming less than 20% of tokens used by comparable methods. This study presents an efficient, training-free paradigm for RAG enhancement that effectively optimizes retrieval quality while maintaining high recall. Our code is released at https://github.com/BUPT-Reasoning-Lab/NeocorRAG.

</details>


### [194] [SimEval-IR: A Unified Toolkit and Benchmark Suite for Evaluating User Simulators and Search Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27878)
*Saber Zerhoudi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SimEval-IR 是一个开源工具包和基准套件，用于标准化评估交互式信息检索中的用户模拟器，区分行为真实性和测试者可靠性两个目标，并发现分类器判别器（"类人性"检验）对系统排名有效性的预测能力极弱（r=+0.09），而边际点击深度距离和会话嵌入上的 Fréchet 距离信号更强（|r|=0.43 和 0.40）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户模拟器在交互式信息检索中日益重要，但社区缺乏标准化的评估工具；现有评估常混淆行为真实性（匹配真实用户行为）和测试者可靠性（产生有效系统排名）这两个不同且有时冲突的目标。

Method: 作者构建了 SimEval-IR 工具包，包含：(1) 统一的会话模式，涵盖会话搜索和对话式交互，带有验证的数据集适配器和显式损失核算；(2) 三个可执行基准测试，分别覆盖行为真实性、测试者可靠性（RATE风格估计）以及两者关联分析；(3) 在四种真实数据集（两种语言）和四种模拟器系列上提供基线结果，并通过相关性分析检验不同真实性指标对系统排名有效性的预测能力。

Result: 分类器判别器（"类人性"检验）对系统排名有效性几乎没有池化预测能力（r=+0.09, n=48）；边际点击深度距离和会话嵌入上的 Fréchet 距离则提供了更强的信号（|r|=0.43 和 0.40, p≤0.005）。

Conclusion: SimEval-IR 为社区提供了标准化评估工具，揭示了现有主流真实性测试（类人性判别器）与模拟器实际测试可靠性之间的脱节，并指向了更具预测力的替代指标。

Abstract: User simulators are increasingly central to interactive information retrieval, yet the community lacks standardized evaluation tools. Simulators serve two objectives, behavioral realism (matching real user behavior) and tester reliability (producing valid system rankings), and these are often conflated despite being distinct and sometimes conflicting. We present SimEval-IR, an open-source toolkit and benchmark suite that makes this distinction measurable. SimEval-IR provides: (1) a canonical session schema unifying session search and conversational interactions, with validated dataset adapters and explicit loss accounting; (2) three executable benchmarks covering behavioral realism, tester reliability with RATE-style estimation, and an analysis linking the two; and (3) baseline results across four real datasets in two languages and four simulator families. Our key finding: the classifier-discriminator ''human-likeness'' check, the dominant realism test in the literature, has essentially no pooled predictive power for system-ranking validity ($r{=}{+}0.09$, $n{=}48$), while marginal click-depth distance and Fréchet distance over session embeddings give a much stronger signal ($|r|{=}0.43$ and $0.40$, $p{\leq}0.005$). SimEval-IR is released with all configurations and scripts to reproduce the reported analysis.

</details>


### [195] [Efficient Multivector Retrieval with Token-Aware Clustering and Hierarchical Indexing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28142)
*Silvio Martinico,Franco Maria Nardini,Cosimo Rulli,Rossano Venturini*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TACHIOM是一种利用token级结构加速多向量检索的系统，通过考虑token分布的质心分配方法，支持百万级质心，结合基于图的索引和优化乘积量化，相比k-means聚类速度提升247倍，检索速度提升9.8倍，同时保持相当或更优的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多向量检索模型虽效果好，但部署时计算和存储成本高；现有基于k-means的解决方案在聚类数量和数据量大时扩展性差，且偏向高频token，低估了稀有但具区分性的token。

Method: TACHIOM利用token级结构，通过考虑token分布的质心分配方法扩展到百万级质心；结合基于图的质心索引和优化的乘积量化（Product Quantization）布局，实现高效最终评分，避免昂贵的token级计算。

Result: 在MS-MARCOv1和LoTTE数据集上，TACHIOM相比k-means聚类速度提升247倍，相比现有最优系统检索速度提升9.8倍，同时保持相当或更优的有效性。

Conclusion: TACHIOM通过利用token分布特性和高效的索引结构，在显著提升多向量检索系统效率的同时，保持了模型的高有效性。

Abstract: Multivector retrieval models achieve state-of-the-art effectiveness through fine-grained token-level representations, but their deployment incurs substantial computational and memory costs. Current solutions, based on the well-known k-means clustering algorithm, group similar vectors together to enable both effective compression and efficient retrieval. However, standard k-means scales poorly with the number of clusters and dataset size, and favours frequent tokens during training while underrepresenting rare, discriminative ones. In this work, we introduce TACHIOM, a multivector retrieval system that exploits token-level structure to significantly accelerate both clustering and retrieval. By accounting for tokens' distribution during centroid allocation, TACHIOM easily scales to millions of centroids, enabling highly accurate document scoring using only centroids, avoiding expensive token-level computation. TACHIOM combines a graph-based index over centroids with an optimized Product Quantization layout for efficient final scoring. Experiments on MS-MARCOv1 and LoTTE show that TACHIOM achieves up to $247\times$ faster clustering than k-means and up to $9.8\times$ retrieval speedup over state-of-the-art systems while maintaining comparable or superior effectiveness.

</details>
